declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %r = call i32 @"example.com/stdtest/io.will$main"()
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %r
}
